### API, ordering and staleness

- **AC-43a** **GIVEN** the hooks after BOOTING, **THEN** one Emitter hook `emitter.place` at divisor 1, offset 0 with `after: [body.integrate, graph.propagate.failure]`, the two `after` edges carrying `tolerates_stale` false (`body.integrate`) and true (`graph.propagate.failure`) (ADR-0017 D3.1). **AC-43b** on a Graph tick it reads the node states and descriptors that run produced. **AC-43c** an unknown hook id or a cycle fails boot. `(auto)` [EM-1, EM-13, EM-17]
- **AC-44** **GIVEN** `set_switch` at tick 121, **WHEN** readers are sampled at 126, **THEN** an `after: [emitter.place]` hook sees the lamp lit, P2 `validate` sees 125's table (unlit), `suit.vitals` reads the beam `lit` as of 125 and `validate` at 127 sees lit. `(auto)` [D.13, EM-25]
- **AC-45a** **GIVEN** a mutator called outside a `WriteScope`, **THEN** the Diagnostics seam records an assert report when `build_is_debug` is true, and the call is rejected with no state change when it is false (the two flag states are two runs of the same test). **AC-45b** Class B mutators clamp (`set_load_request` above `req_max`, `spawn_deployable` past the cap) and never reject; an unknown id returns 0 or false. **AC-45c** `toggle_beam`, `set_switch` and `set_beacon` match the Commands table and every submitted command reaches exactly one terminal result. `(auto)` [EM-5, EM-7, CR-5, CR-9] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-46a** **GIVEN** a beam sweeping 192 subjects for 60 ticks with a fixed output, **THEN** no dirty key fires on any tick (pose never dirties); **AND** on the tick the beam flips `switch_on` exactly one key `E/<beam id>` fires; **AND** in S3 (32 static emitters to 0) exactly 32 keys fire in that tick, every key is `E/<emitter id>` (`(KIND << 32) | id`), none names a subject, and the count is at most `MAX_EMITTERS` 96 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. **AC-46c** **GIVEN** the beam on with the shipped spill, **THEN** `beam_spill(beam_id)` returns `{kind BEAM, shape_id 11, origin_px` equal to the foot origin, `reach_px` 24, `r_lit_px` 18, `r_off_px` 19, `output_pm` 600, `curve_class` SOFT, `priority` 255`}` (recomputed at R 24, output 600: `c`(18) = ⌊600 × 250 / 1000⌋ = 150 ≥ 150 and `c`(19) = ⌊600 × 208 / 1000⌋ = 124 < 150, so `r_lit_px` 18; `c`(19) = 124 ≥ 120 and `c`(20) = ⌊600 × 166 / 1000⌋ = 99 < 120, so `r_off_px` 19) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*, `emitters()` holds no record with `shape_id` 11 and its length is unchanged by the spill, `section_emission` is unchanged, and `beam_spill` returns null with the beam off or `BEAM_SPILL_RADIUS_PX` 0; in an otherwise dark room a subject already lit at d 19 stays lit and one at d 20 goes unlit, and a Lighting-side fake that draws the pool to 19 px (r_draw = max(19, 8)) agrees with the sim: an object 1 px inside `r_off_px` is lit and drawn inside the pool, one 1 px outside is unlit *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* (the sim half is `(auto)`; the rendered half joins AC-56, `(spike, manual)`). `(auto)` [EM-26, EM-6] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **AC-46b** each emitter exposes exactly `id, kind, subkind, shape_id, origin_px, aim_units, half_angle_units, reach_px, r_lit_px, r_off_px, curve_class, output_pm, hue_class, lit, section_id, body_id, priority` (17 fields) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*, plus `shapes()` and the one derived-record accessor `beam_spill(beam_id)` (AC-46c; it returns a view under the no-retain rule and allocates no record copy), and no public function mutates a record *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. `(auto, lint)` [EM-26, CR-10] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-47** **GIVEN** `emitter.state.lit` on the seat's beam, **THEN** it is true whenever `output_pm > 0` for any kind, and `lit(ref)` is the hysteresis answer; both names exist unchanged. `(auto)` [EM-2]
- **AC-65** **GIVEN** `instantiate_section_emitters(section)` then `discard_section_emitters(section)`, **THEN** the first creates exactly the authored fixtures, lanterns and beacon with complete derived fields (an unknown section is a no-op) and the second removes them, any glowsticks in the section and its OBJECT subjects (ACTOR and HORROR subjects remain). `(auto)` [EM-5, E1, E2]
- **AC-67** **GIVEN** `set_beacon` with origin `PLAYER`, **THEN** rejected (`ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED`); with `SYSTEM` on a FIXTURE id, `NOT_BEACON`; on a non-existent id, `NO_SUCH_EMITTER`; **one row per beacon-node state**: on a missing, tombstoned, dormant, `4 SALVAGED` or `5 ABSENT` node, `NOT_LIVE`; on a `2 FAILED` or `3 DESTROYED` node, on a dead bus (`served_pm` 0) and on a healthy node, accepted (the first three go STARVED with `switch_on` set, `output_pm` 0). `(auto)` [EM-7, EM-13] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*

### Boot invariants

- **AC-48** **GIVEN** `EDGE_FLOOR_PM` 160 with `LIT_ON_PM` 300 and `BEAM_BASE_PM` 1000, **WHEN** the data loads, **THEN** boot fails I-EDGE; (250, 150) passes. `(auto)` [D.15]
- **AC-49** **GIVEN** `LIT_OFF_PM` zero or at least `LIT_ON_PM`, **THEN** boot fails I-HYST. `(auto)` [D.15]
- **AC-50** **GIVEN** `SPLIT_AXIS_UNITS` 205, **THEN** boot fails I-WEDGE (182 < 183); 206 passes. `(auto)` [D.15]
- **AC-51a** **GIVEN** the boot tables, **THEN** every product `D²`, `d2 × COS2_Q28`, segment cross-product and `T × AIM_UNITS` for `T` = 2^50 − 1 is below 2^62. **AC-51b** `T` = 2^50 fails the assertion. **AC-51c** `DIR[(2048 − a) mod 4096] = (−ux, uy)` for all 4096 angles (I-MIRROR). `(auto)` [D.15]
- **AC-52** *(one row per case; each row is its own GIVEN / THEN and passes or fails alone)* `(auto, lint)` [D.15] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the first draft packed about twenty cases into one criterion; rows 52-17 and 52-18 are new)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: a **Result** column is added; REFUSE = boot or build is refused with a fault code, WARN = a warning is reported and boot proceeds, FALLBACK = a run-time fallback with a counter, boot proceeds, PASS = no fault. Rows 52-19 to 52-21 are new.)*

| Row | GIVEN (data) | Result | THEN |
|---|---|---|---|
| 52-1 | authored emitters = `MAX_EMITTERS` − 26 + 1 (the 26 is the I-CAPS (a) reserve: fault glow 10 + deployables 8 + evicting 4 + bound seats 4), i.e. 71 at the defaults | REFUSE | boot fails I-CAPS (a); `MAX_EMITTERS` − 26 (70) passes. *This is the lint ceiling (arithmetic), not the content count: the MVP content authors at most 50 (28 fixtures + 20 lanterns + 2 beacon slots; the registry authors 1 beacon), AC-58* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |

### Performance

- **AC-64** **GIVEN** unit costs `W_REJ` 10, `W_CONE` 20, `W_CON` 63, `W_SEG` 40, `K_OCC` 4, `W_RECTS` 30, `W_POSE` 100, `W_DER` 8, `W_TAB` 4, `W_PUB` 15, `W_DELTA` 8, `W_BROAD` 30, `W_CELL` 2 (ADR-0017 D12.6; `W_SNAP` retired), `W_RLIT` 230, `W_FRAME` 2 *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*, **WHEN** the counted-work function is evaluated with the fixture's recorded cell count `N_cells`, **THEN** *(Amended 2026-10-29 — sync knock-on fix: AC-64 gates the formula's structure and the cell-free bases; the pre-retirement totals 9,989, 12,608, 21,470, 28,106, 11,189, 10,202, 12,261, 19,621, 13,965, 20,841, 13,397, 151,430, 181,248 and 12,969 are reference values only and are not asserted)* the function has a `T_cell = N_cells × W_CELL` term and no `T_snap` term (`EmitterUnits` carries `CELL` and not `SNAP`, ADR-0017 D12.1), and the typical design point (174 subjects, 34 emitters, `M` 3) gives `T_dyn` 4,336, `T_mov` 2,457, `T_der` 472, `T_tab` 696, `T_pub` 90, `T_broad` 150, `T_rlit` 1,380, `T_cell` = `N_cells` × 2, and `T_mand` = **9,581 + `T_cell`**; the movers-at-the-bound point (`M` 6, `N` 192) gives 12,200 + `T_cell`; the VS-B four-beam configuration gives 21,026 + `T_cell`; the worst corner (all caps, `M` 16) gives 26,954 + `T_cell`; the scenarios S1 to S5 add 1,200, 213, 2,272, 9,632 and 3,976 plus each scenario's own cell term (cell-free tick totals 10,781, 9,794, 11,853, 19,213 and 13,557) against naive counterparts of 11,522, 11,522, 143,202, 143,202 and 26,336; the weld and cut scenarios S8 add 10,852 and 3,408 plus their cell terms (cell-free tick totals 20,433 and 12,989) against a naive 143,202 each; the cold build S6 (and the S8b fallback) is 151,022 + its cell term (180,096 + its cell term at the ceilings); a linear scan at `N` 174 gives 12,561 + its cell term and exceeds `B` 10,000 (it fits only up to `N` = 67 before cells); `N_cells` is not asserted as a constant: it is the value the fixture's counting test records, so a change of `EMITTER_CELL_PX` changes the recorded `N_cells` and `T_cell` and no other term *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. `(auto)` **AC-64b** **GIVEN** the fixture scene `tests/fixtures/emitter/exposure_dp.json` (authored for this criterion: 32 static emitters, a beam and a beacon, 174 subjects of which 3 move, positioned so that the broadphase yields exactly 30 / 24 / 8 candidates, radius survivors and cone survivors for the beam, 20 / 16 / 4 for the beacon, and `E_cand` 6 and `E_surv` 3 for every static subject, **and a recorded `N_cells` per tick and per scenario at the fixture's `EMITTER_CELL_PX`** *(Amended 2026-10-29 — sync knock-on fix: the counter's `CELL` operation, one count per grid cell walked by a candidate query (ADR-0017 D12.1, D12.6), is a counted operation of this criterion and the formula's `T_cell` term is what it is compared against)*; the fixture is itself verified by a counting test before AC-64b may use it) and the implementation's work counter (`emitter_work_units`, incremented by the same `W_` constants at each counted operation), injected through a `WorkCounter` port, with `NullWorkCounter` in every shipped and benchmarked build (ADR-0010 section 4.4; per-operation counting perturbs the Release benchmark, so the AC-53 build injects `NullWorkCounter` and the benchmark never runs with the counting double) *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0010 sync)*, **WHEN** the scripted scenarios run on the fakes of the test seams, **THEN** the counter equals the formula's value exactly, `T_cell` included, for the typical tick and for S1 to S6 and S8 (the formula evaluated with the fixture's recorded `N_cells`; the pre-retirement totals of AC-64 are not the expectation), is identical on two machines, and a counter that disagrees with the formula fails the build (the model cannot drift from the code); a fixture that does not reproduce the stated counts fails its own counting test first. `(auto)` [D.14, EM-24] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the first draft tested arithmetic only)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-53** **GIVEN** a scripted benchmark on a release export (Godot 4.7.2 release export template, `forward_plus`, vsync on, 120-frame warm-up, startup read-back recorded) on RM-DESKTOP and on RM-DECK (delegated ruling R12; Open Question 28, resolved) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*, **WHEN** `emitter.place` and all exposure evaluation run for the design point (the typical design point: 174 subjects, moving beam, one beacon, three movers; the movers-at-the-bound point with six movers and 192 subjects is recorded separately) and for S1 door travel, S2 one-room blackout, S3 ship blackout, S4 ship restore with inrush, S5 eight-stick wane, S6 cold restore, S8 weld and cut (and S8b when Layout reports `ALL`) and the VS-B four-beam configuration (S7 is removed: nothing is instantiated after `init_world`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*, **THEN** the report lists P50, P99, P99.9 and max per scenario and the verdicts follow the decision table of D.14: steady-state design point P99 ≤ 0.4 ms (`EXPOSURE_BUDGET_MS`, with the machine's own `B` from its measured `U_OP_NS`), S1 to S5 and S8 max ≤ 1.0 ms (`EXPOSURE_TICK_CEILING_MS`; S1, S2 and S8 exceed `B` by design for their one tick or while a door moves, and the overshoot is recorded), S6 and S8b ≤ 8 ms (`COLD_REBUILD_CEILING_MS`); the worst corner is recorded against 26,954 units + `T_cell` (the cell-free base; the pre-retirement 28,106 included `T_snap`) whatever the result, and the VS-B configuration against 21,026 + `T_cell` (pre-retirement 21,470) where it is expected to fail (Open Question 27); the benchmark runs with `NullWorkCounter` injected *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0010 sync)*. Until EM-SPIKE-1 has run on both machines the criterion is NOT ASSESSED (pending spike measurement on RM-DESKTOP / RM-DECK), never passed. `(spike)` [D.14, EM-24] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: P99 alone hid rare hitches, no hardware was named, and the rebuild scenarios were unpriced)*
- **AC-54** **GIVEN** the same scripted ticks (the design point and S1 to S6 and S8) on two machines, **THEN** every subject's `lux_pm`, `act_lux_pm`, `lit` and `top_emitter_id`, the `emitter_work_units` of every tick and `state_hash` are identical (counted units, never wall-clock); **AND GIVEN** a snapshot taken at each tick of S1 to S5 and S8 and restored, **THEN** every answer from T+1 on equals the uninterrupted run (no answer depends on work counted in an earlier tick, which is what the deferred probe budget violated). `(auto)` [EM-24, EM-28, G4] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*

### Sim/render agreement (needs 2D Lighting & Occlusion)

- **AC-55** **GIVEN** a rendered hard-edged cone at every zoom rung 1..`k_auto`+2, **WHEN** a subject is 1 px inside and 1 px outside the drawn edge, **THEN** the HUD prompt is present then absent. Evidence: `production/qa/evidence/em-edge-zoom-[rung].png`. `(spike, manual)` [P1, EM-18]
- **AC-56** **GIVEN** a rendered soft pool at threshold alpha at full modulation on a bright plate, **THEN** its radius is `max(r_off_px, POOL_RESIDUAL_PX)` ± 1 px (equal to `r_off_px` ± 1 px whenever `r_off_px` ≥ 8; below that Lighting draws its 8 px residual, never smaller than where a prompt lives); **AND** on withdrawal the prompt of an already-lit subject persists exactly to that edge (± 1 px) and never beyond; **AND** on approach the prompt of a not-yet-lit subject first appears at `r_lit_px` ± 1 px, `r_off_px − r_lit_px` px inside the edge (the sim values are exact, so the closed form's own 1 px error no longer consumes the tolerance; the mid-Graphite tolerance is Lighting's AC-LO-8); each hard-edged row's rendered reach equals the table ± 1 px. Evidence: `em-pool-edge-[rung].png`. `(spike, lint)` [D.7, D.8, EM-26] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*
- **AC-57** **GIVEN** a Light2D shadow origin at the chest and the sim segment at the foot (outcome A of 2D Lighting LO-10, or outcome B if its spike decouples them), **WHEN** a player stands `s` px south of the first legal row in front of a north wall for `s` in {1, 10, 20, 34, 44} (the 1–44 px band of EM-6), with the beam aimed N, S, E and W, for every `CHEST_POINT` row (idle, walk, run, carry, work, stagger, down), **THEN** the drawn beam and the sim agree to ≤ 2 base px on whether the wall blocks the cone, measured as zero mismatching cells at Chebyshev radius 2 from any simulation boundary (the radius-1 count is recorded beside it; ADR-0018 section 2, SO-2) *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0018 sync)* — one table row per (`s`, aim, `CHEST_POINT` row), each a pass or a recorded failure — and **no** row shows a beam that renders black while the sim reports it lit; a failing row selects outcome D (EM-6). Lighting spike step 3b is a pre-implementation gate for ADR candidate 5. Evidence: `production/qa/evidence/em-shadow-origin-[s]-[aim]-[date].png`. `(spike, manual)` [EM-6, ADR candidate 5] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the first draft's band was wrong and the criterion could not fail)*
- **AC-86** **GIVEN** a stub Viewport whose visible rect and zoom rung vary over rungs 1..`k_auto`+2 (the sim itself has no zoom input) and the same 600-tick command stream, **THEN** `state_hash`, every `lux_pm`, `lit` and `top_emitter_id` are identical at every rung; **AND GIVEN** a subject placed outside the stub's visible rect in a room lit by a lamp, **THEN** it reads `lit` true and its lamp is counted in `section_emission` at every rung; **AND** a lint finds no camera, zoom or viewport symbol in `src/sim/emitter/`. `(auto, lint)` [EM-30] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*

### MVP content and cross-system

- **AC-58** **GIVEN** the MVP slice data files, **WHEN** the content lint runs, **THEN** seat beams = 1 (table sized 4); **authored emitters ≤ 50** (the per-kind caps 28 fixtures + 20 lanterns + 2 beacons; the MVP content authors 1 beacon, and the caps are ceilings, not targets) **and the I-CAPS (a) total authored + 26 (fault glow 10, deployables 8, evicting 4, seats 4) ≤ `MAX_EMITTERS`** (at the caps 50 + 26 = 76 ≤ 96) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; authored subjects ≤ 192; exactly one self-powered cryo-bay lamp (EMERGENCY), exactly one self-powered DOCK_STATUS glow per dock room and no other `node_ref` 0 fixture *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings: X2)*; exactly one beacon; every record has a non-null `priority`; in-view counts per compartment ≤ 6 shadow-casting / 24 non-shadow / 8 lanterns. Fixture counts are exact per registry `mvp_fixture_counts`, **each plus one DOCK_STATUS per dock room, to be recounted by Derelict Authoring DA-22 `[PLACEHOLDER — derive at the DA-22 recount; the 28-fixture cap holds while the largest loaded set stays at or below 28 − its dock rooms]`** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings: X2)* (before that recount: home_ship 8 = 7 powered lamps + 1 EMERGENCY, derelict_a 6, derelict_b 7; loaded sets 8 / 14 / 15 / 15 / 21, all ≤ 28; lanterns ≤ 20 per world, 8 authored; beacon exactly 1); the lint fails any other count. `(lint, review)` [R24, R32, EM-26]
- **AC-59a** **GIVEN** an object whose anchor is 1 px inside then 1 px outside the rim of a full-output beam, **THEN** `lit(ref)` is true then false at the same tick. `(auto)` **AC-59b** through Interaction, `validate` accepts then rejects `NOT_LIT` and the HUD prompt follows `lit(ref)` only. Evidence: `em-prompt-edge-[date].png`. `(auto-int against Interaction's `validate` and the HUD prompt query; manual for the screenshot; BLOCKING, Interaction is designed)` [PC-6, EM-22] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-60** **GIVEN** the hook registrations of the designed Attention (`attention.step`) and Horror (`horror.step`), **THEN** Attention declares `after` `graph.propagate.power` and `emitter.place`, Horror declares `after` Attention and `emitter.place`, and a lit lamp counts once as light (`output_pm` × `reach_px`) and not also as `lamps_lit_w` in Attention's intake. `(lint over the registrations; auto for the single count)` [EM-25, EM-27] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-61** **GIVEN** the Cross-References refresh rows tagged OWED and the registry, **WHEN** the tracking script `tools/ci/check_crossrefs` runs (for each OWED row it greps the target file for the row's quoted refreshed string and reports resolved or unresolved; for the registry it checks that `referenced_by` lists this GDD for each entry it owns, that every ★ constant is registered with a `[PLACEHOLDER]` tag and an owner, and that no tick-denominated glowstick life constant exists), **THEN** the report is produced and lists every unresolved row by target file; the criterion passes when the script runs and the unresolved list is empty, and until then it is an ADVISORY tracking report (the owners of the sibling files close the rows), never a gate on this GDD's stories. `(lint, review, ADVISORY)` [Cross-References, Tuning Knobs] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-FEEL** *(rewritten; each clause now names its measurable half and the sign-off is a yes/no per clause)* **GIVEN** a launched build with a frame-step capture, **WHEN** the technical-artist and game-designer play the toggle, the lamp-bank inrush dip, the rim at rest and the glowstick wane, **THEN** each signs yes or no, with a retained screenshot series, to: (1) **toggle** — the sim commits in the apply tick (0 ticks, proved by AC-12a and AC-32) and in the frame-step series the drawn light matches the committed state on every frame except at most the one render frame after the commit (the presenter's allowance); a pre-played click with no light change is shown for a rejected toggle; (2) **inrush** — the visible dip of the lamp bank is seen and reads as the ship straining, not a flicker bug (the numbers are the Graph's: the 945 permille for five runs is a Graph integration test, AC-113, not a feel criterion); (3) **rim at rest** — AC-55's edge is judged by eye at each rung; (4) **glowstick wane** — the shrink reads as a steady slow loss (the numbers are AC-35 and AC-36c2). Evidence: `production/qa/evidence/em-toggle-feel-[date].png`, `em-inrush-feel-[date].png`, `em-wane-feel-[date].png` and a sign-off note listing the four yes/no results. `(manual)` [Game Feel] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*

### Gaps / untestable as written

- The sim/render agreement (AC-55 to AC-57, AC-59b) needs 2D Lighting & Occlusion; until then the edge agreement is tested at rest in the sim only.
- `EXPOSURE_BUDGET_MS`, the work-unit costs and `U_OP_NS` are model parameters until the GDScript spike; AC-53 is the first measurement. The counted-work arithmetic (AC-64) is deterministic and automated now.
- Whether Light2D can decouple a shadow origin from its texture offset in 4.7.2 is not sourceable from the pinned reference; AC-57 decides it.
- Glowstick numbers, lantern bases and `lantern_on_states` are Item DB placeholders; the Graph's `run_pm` read now exists (LS-19 additions), so AC-34b is `(auto-int)`; AC-35 follows the Item DB numbers. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- AC-41 is unblocked: the Graph now honours the `post_restore` rebuild (LS-21 and its restore edge case); the `served_pm_last` fallback is retired. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- AC-14b, AC-25c and AC-59b depend on Interaction (PC-7 re-check schedule); "within 6 ticks" in the draft was replaced by one number: the abort is at the first 6-tick re-check after the dark tick, at most five dark channel ticks.
- Post-MVP backlog, not yet written as criteria (each is covered in prose by the rule named; the MVP behaviours formerly listed here are now AC-104 to AC-113) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: `unregister_subject` and `set_subject_anchor` one-tick lag (E7, E8); same-state `SYSTEM` `set_switch` / `set_beacon` emit no dirty key (EM-7); read-view purity under repeated calls (EM-25); records complete from P4 of their creation tick (EM-5); beam output constant with charge (EM-8); inrush dip of 945 permille for five Graph runs on eight lamps, a Graph integration number (D.2); the near-field blind band when the spill is switched off with `BEAM_SPILL_RADIUS_PX` 0 (EM-6; the 0 branch is covered only by AC-10b's boundary case) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; invalid state transitions reported to diagnostics (States and Transitions); descriptor without a glow row and the `_msk` overlay (EM-17); Item DB def-to-record lint (EM-4, EM-11); `MIRROR` never ticks the hook, deferred to VS-B (EM-1). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- Feel (the edge of the light, the toggle) is not automated: reviewed by the technical-artist and game-designer in a launched build with retained screenshots (AC-FEEL).
- **Smoke scenario** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: S-EM-1, run by `/smoke-check` on the Derelict A fixture in a launched build, retaining a screenshot under `production/qa/evidence/` for each visual step: (1) beam on, a valve 1 px inside the drawn edge prompts and one 1 px outside does not; (2) two `toggle_beam` presses inside 12 ticks produce one flip (screenshot of the beam after the second press, plus the log line of the `TOO_SOON` rejection), and the foot spill at 24 px lights an item underfoot only while the beam is on *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; (3) a glowstick thrown into the next room blooms at landing and shrinks; (4) the lamp bank is switched off and the room's prompts vanish on the tick the outputs reach 0; (5) a save and restore keeps `state_hash` (evidence is the logged hash before and after, **not a screenshot**: a hash cannot be photographed) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; (6) walking through a door moves the beam's `section_emission` to the next section on the crossing tick (evidence: the logged `section_emission` of both sections on the crossing tick, plus a screenshot on each side of the door).
- **Playtest metric stub** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; the protocol is the qa-lead's)*: (a) *dark-at-feet*: the share of lit-beam ticks in which an interactable within `SPILL_METRIC_BAND_PX` of the feet is unlit, where the band is **40 px, the largest `r_lit_px` any legal spill value can produce** (EM-6), so the metric can actually move when `BEAM_SPILL_RADIUS_PX` moves; measured over Interaction's whole `REACH_PX` 72 it would be dominated by the 41–72 px band that no spill setting reaches, and it has a floor built in, because every object inside the spill's own `r_lit_px` is lit by construction while the beam is on (the same share over `REACH_PX` is recorded as a diagnostic, not as the decision metric) (the metric that decides Open Question 17; placeholder decision line 5 % of lit-beam ticks) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* (release telemetry counters `emitter_lit_beam_ticks` and `emitter_dark_at_feet_ticks`, incremented by the sim with no gameplay effect, supply it); (b) *broken-prompt reports*: testers are asked after each sortie whether a prompt ever felt broken; (c) beam toggles per minute and `TOO_SOON` rejections per hour (strobe behaviour); (d) glowsticks thrown per sortie; (e) the share of sortie time spent with the beam off. **Telemetry counters** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* (release counters, incremented by the sim, no gameplay effect): `emitter_lit_beam_ticks`, `emitter_dark_at_feet_ticks` (a), `emitter_beam_toggles` and `emitter_too_soon` (c), `emitter_glowsticks_thrown` (d), `emitter_beam_off_ticks` (e); the broken-prompt report (b) is a survey answer, not a counter.

## Open Questions

*Every rule, formula and cap of this document is decided; none of those is listed here. Items 1–4 were the user-facing decisions this run took provisionally in autonomous mode and logged; the creative-director's recommendation on each (keep, CD-GDD-ALIGN 2026-10-26) is recorded in the row. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: rows 1–4, 17 and 23 were marked as user decisions.)* **Rulings of 2026-10-27 (design-review decisions):** rows 1–4 are resolved by user ruling (keep, D20–D23; the provisional flags are cleared), row 17 by user ruling (`BEAM_SPILL_RADIUS_PX` 24, D24), row 23 by a default applied confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20) (D25), row 25 in part by a default applied, confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20) (D32), and row 28 resolved 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R12: RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK, release export). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)* Items 5–29 are open for a named owner and target. "GDD" as an owner means the named GDD must adopt or counter the stated text when it lands.*
